use super::*;
use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleHop, MAX_RULE_HOPS};
// @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — bounded live, authorized point reads are distinct
// from the immutable audience capture of read through.
pub(super) fn rule_reference(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, base: &Expr, field: &str, bindings: &RuleBindings, site: &Site) -> Fallible<(RuleTerm, Ty)> {
    let refusal = |reason: &str| Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP", reason,
        reference_rewrite(catalog, info, &Expr::Member { base: Box::new(base.clone()), name: field.into(), site: site.clone() })).guide("rules");
    let (source, ty) = match base {
        Expr::Member { .. } => rule_term(catalog, info, base, bindings)?,
        _ => return Err(refusal("a rule hop needs a row-local reference or an id lookup through it")),
    };
    let (column, old, mut hops) = match source {
        RuleTerm::Field(c) | RuleTerm::NextField(c) => (c, false, vec![]),
        RuleTerm::OldField(c) => (c, true, vec![]),
        RuleTerm::Reference { table, column, field, old } => (column, old, vec![RuleHop { table, field }]),
        RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column, hops, old } => (column, old, hops),
        _ => return Err(refusal("a rule hop needs a row-local reference")),
    };
    if hops.len() >= MAX_RULE_HOPS { return Err(refusal("a rule term follows at most three references")); }
    let Ty::Ref(table) = ty.required() else { return Err(type_error(site, "a rule hop needs a table reference", "declare the source column with its referenced table type")); };
    let target = catalog.tables.get(&table).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("unknown table {table}"), "declare the referenced table"))?;
    let (owner, source_field) = hops.last().map_or((info, column.as_str()), |hop| (&catalog.tables[&hop.table], hop.field.as_str()));
    let private = |info: &TableInfo, field: &str| info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == field && c.written_by.is_some() && !c.readable_by_audience);
    if !bindings.hops { return Err(refusal("read rules inherit whole parent authority with read through; live hops belong to write rules")); }
    if target.maintained.is_some() || super::super::columns::fields(target).any(|c| c == field) {
        return Err(refusal("a rule hop cannot read maintained targets or fields at a fixed point-read cost; pass the view's group keys to the mutation"));
    }
    if target.decl.ephemeral.is_some() { return Err(refusal("ephemeral rows cannot decide durable write authority; store this authority in a durable table before using the mutation check")); }
    if private(owner, source_field) || private(target, field) { return Err(refusal("a rule hop cannot disclose a private field to the row audience")); }
    let field_ty = column_ty(target, field).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("{table} has no column {field}"), name_rewrite(field, target.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)))?;
    if hops.is_empty() { return Ok((RuleTerm::Reference { table, column, field: field.into(), old }, field_ty)); }
    hops.push(RuleHop { table, field: field.into() });
    Ok((RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column, hops, old }, field_ty))
}

/// A concrete mutation check. Bind each row before testing the predicate so
/// neither null equality nor negation can turn an absent/hidden hop into a grant.
/// Row-local fields name mutation inputs; named images keep old_/next_ prefixes.
pub(super) fn reference_rewrite(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr) -> String {
    fn lookup(catalog: &Catalog, table: &str, key: &str) -> String {
        // Aggregate rows are addressed by their group, never by a row id.
        let group = catalog.tables.get(table).filter(|t| t.maintained.is_some())
            .and_then(|t| t.indexes.iter().find(|i| i.name == "byGroup"));
        let key = group.map_or_else(|| format!("id = {key}"), |index|
            index.columns.iter().map(|field| format!("{field} = {field}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
        format!("{table}[{key}]")
    }
    fn private_guard(info: &TableInfo, field: &str, row: &str, lines: &mut Vec<String>) {
        if let Some(owner) = info.decl.columns.iter().find(|c| c.name == field && !c.readable_by_audience).and_then(|c| c.written_by.as_ref()) {
            lines.push(format!("require {row}{owner} = viewer else FORBIDDEN"));
        }
    }
    fn selected(catalog: &Catalog, table: &str, field: &str, local: &str) -> (String, Option<Ty>) {
        let target = catalog.tables.get(table);
        // A maintained view lookup returns its scalar value directly.
        let value = if field == "value" && target.is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.is_some()) { local.into() }
            else { format!("{local}.{field}") };
        (value,target.and_then(|t| column_ty(t,field)))
    }
    fn render(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr, lines: &mut Vec<String>) -> (String, Option<Ty>) {
        match expr {
            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => {
                private_guard(info, name, "", lines);
                (name.clone(), column_ty(info, name))
            },
            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Name { .. }) => {
                let Expr::Name { name: image, .. } = base.as_ref() else { unreachable!() };
                if let Some(Ty::Ref(table)) = column_ty(info, image).map(|ty| ty.required()) {
                    let local = format!("rule_parent_{}", lines.len());
                    lines.push(format!("{local} = {} ! else FORBIDDEN", lookup(catalog, &table, image)));
                    if let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&table) { private_guard(target, name, &format!("{local}."), lines); }
                    selected(catalog,&table,name,&local)
                } else { (format!("{image}_{name}"), column_ty(info, name)) }
            }
            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => {
                // @ref LLP 3000.036#14.3 — lane 092: a named unique key (`staff[principal = viewer]`) stays named.
                let mut named = None;
                let (key, ty) = if let Expr::Read(read) = base.as_ref() {
                    let key = read.keys.first().map(|key| render(catalog, info, &key.value, lines).0).unwrap_or_else(|| "id".into());
                    named = read.keys.first().and_then(|key| key.column.clone()).filter(|column| column != "id");
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#18 — preserve the typed positional key; viewer is a principal, never an intrinsic row id.
                    if named.is_none() && key == "viewer" && read.keys.len() == 1 {
                        if let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) {
                            let candidates: Vec<_> = target.indexes.iter().filter(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() == 1
                                && target.columns.get(&index.columns[0]).is_some_and(|ty| ty.kind == TypeKind::Principal)
                                && read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|name| *name == index.name)).collect();
                            if candidates.len() == 1 { named = Some(candidates[0].columns[0].clone()); }
                        }
                    }
                    (key, Some(Ty::Ref(read.table.clone())))
                } else { render(catalog, info, base, lines) };
                if let Some(Ty::Ref(table)) = ty.map(|ty| ty.required()) {
                    let local = format!("rule_parent_{}", lines.len());
                    let target = match &named { Some(column) => format!("{table}[{column} = {key}]"), None if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Read(_)) => format!("{table}[{key}]"), None => lookup(catalog, &table, &key) };
                    lines.push(format!("{local} = {target} ! else FORBIDDEN"));
                    if let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&table) { private_guard(target, name, &format!("{local}."), lines); }
                    selected(catalog,&table,name,&local)
                } else { (crate::print::expression(expr), None) }
            }
            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => {
                let left = render(catalog, info, left, lines).0;
                let right = render(catalog, info, right, lines).0;
                (format!("({left} {op} {right})"), None)
            }
            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => (format!("{op} ({})", render(catalog, info, value, lines).0), None),
            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => {
                let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| {
                    let value = render(catalog, info, &key.value, lines).0;
                    key.column.as_ref().map_or(value.clone(), |col| format!("{col} = {value}"))
                }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                let index = read.index.as_ref().map_or(String::new(), |i| format!(" by {i}"));
                (format!("exists {}[{keys}]{index}", read.table), None)
            }
            _ => (crate::print::expression(expr), None),
        }
    }
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    let (predicate, _) = render(catalog, info, expr, &mut lines);
    lines.push(format!("require {predicate} else FORBIDDEN"));
    lines.join("\n")
}

/// A read rule that hops (`.box.season`) cannot be a partition's audience.
/// The repair is always one that compiles as printed (round-tripped in
/// `tests/wave2_rule_language.rs` and `tests/wave3_rule_language.rs`): an
/// online-only table or a view probes the row that holds the deciding value
/// (`exists parent[.ref, field = viewer]`, copying the last reference when the
/// chain is longer than one hop); a synced table compared with `viewer`
/// copies the deciding principal, which sync can use as a column audience.
/// Every hop in the rule is repaired, not only the first (lane 033).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — app-farm pattern 33: show a table-rule repair, not mutation syntax.
// @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — wave 2 R9: skip the intermediate one-hop form that is itself refused.
// @ref LLP 3000.036#13.5 — wave 3 #17: repair each hop, and say `read through` widens the audience.
pub(super) fn read_hop_rewrite(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, rule: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
    if let Some(rewrite) = stored_comparison_rewrite(catalog, info, rule) {
        return Some(rewrite);
    }
    let first = single_hop_rewrite(catalog, info, rule)?;
    let quoted = |text: &str| text.split('`').skip(1).step_by(2).map(str::to_owned).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let rule_of = |text: &str| -> Option<Expr> {
        let read = quoted(text).into_iter().find_map(|piece| piece.strip_prefix("read <- ").map(str::to_owned))?;
        let (document, errors) = crate::parser::parse_document("rule.q", &format!("table {}:\n  read <- {read}\n", info.decl.name));
        if !errors.is_empty() { return None; }
        document.tables.first()?.rules.iter().find(|r| r.effect == Effect::Read).map(|r| r.value.clone())
    };
    let mut steps = vec![first.clone()];
    let mut current = rule_of(&first);
    while let Some(next) = current.as_ref().filter(|next| find(next).is_some()) {
        if steps.len() >= MAX_RULE_HOPS * 4 { return None; }
        let step = single_hop_rewrite(catalog, info, next)?;
        current = rule_of(&step);
        steps.push(step);
    }
    if steps.len() == 1 { return Some(first); }
    // Several hops name several audiences; sync delivers one (E_SYNC). The
    // online-only form keeps every reader with a probe per hop.
    if info.maintained.is_none() && info.decl.sync.is_some() && !info.decl.online_only {
        let mut online = info.clone();
        online.decl.sync = None;
        online.decl.online_only = true;
        let repair = read_hop_rewrite(catalog, &online, rule)?;
        return Some(format!("these hops name more than one audience, and a synced table has one (E_SYNC): replace its sync line with `online only`; then {repair}"));
    }
    // Merge the steps' declarations, insert checks and view groups into one repair.
    let (mut declare, mut immutable, mut checks, mut by, mut caveats) = (Vec::<String>::new(), Vec::<String>::new(), Vec::<String>::new(), Vec::<String>::new(), Vec::<String>::new());
    let mut through = None;
    for step in &steps {
        for piece in quoted(step) {
            if let Some(check) = piece.strip_prefix("insert <- … and ") { if !checks.contains(&check.to_owned()) { checks.push(check.into()); } }
            else if let Some(columns) = piece.strip_prefix("immutable ") { for column in columns.split(", ") { if !immutable.iter().any(|c| c == column) { immutable.push(column.into()); } } }
            else if let Some(columns) = piece.strip_prefix("by ") { for column in columns.split(", ") { if !by.iter().any(|c| c == column) { by.push(column.into()); } } }
            else if piece.starts_with("read through") { through.get_or_insert(piece); }
            else if piece.split_once(": ").is_some_and(|(name, ty)| name.chars().all(char::is_alphanumeric) && !ty.contains('[')) && !declare.contains(&piece) { declare.push(piece); }
        }
        if let Some((_, rest)) = step.split_once("so this holds while ") && let Some((columns, _)) = rest.split_once(" cannot change") {
            for column in columns.split(" and ") { if !caveats.iter().any(|c| c == column) { caveats.push(column.into()); } }
        }
    }
    let read = quoted(steps.last()?).into_iter().find(|piece| piece.starts_with("read <- "))?;
    let table = &info.decl.name;
pub fn bind_rule(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, rule: &RuleDecl) -> Fallible<RuleExpr> {
    let bindings = RuleBindings { old: rule.bindings.first().cloned(), next: rule.bindings.get(1).cloned(), update: rule.effect == Effect::Update, insert: rule.effect == Effect::Insert, hops: rule.effect != Effect::Read, present: Vec::new() };
    rule_expr(catalog, info, &rule.value, &bindings).map_err(|error| {
        let mut error = super::first_paste::inherited_rule(catalog, info, rule).unwrap_or(error);
        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR" && rule.effect == Effect::Update && rule.bindings.is_empty()
            && error.rewrite == "write this as a named transition" {
            let source = super::rule_operator::named_update_source(&rule.value);
            error.rewrite = format!("name both row images: `{source}`");
            error.source_rewrite = Some(source);
            error.source_rewrite_kind = Some(crate::diag::SourceRewriteKind::NamedUpdate);
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.6 — the named form of a whole unnamed update rule, so the rewrite keeps every other clause.
        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR" && rule.effect == Effect::Update && rule.bindings.is_empty() && error.rewrite.starts_with("name both images") {
            let comparison = error.message.split('`').nth(1).unwrap_or_default().to_owned();
            error.rewrite = super::rule_operator::named_update(&info.decl.name, &rule.value, &comparison);
        }
        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP" {
            error.rewrite = match rule.effect {
                Effect::Read => reference::read_hop_rewrite(catalog, info, &rule.value).unwrap_or_else(|| "a read rule probes one hop: copy the deciding value onto this row (immutable, checked against the parent by the insert rule), or use read through .parent".into()),
                _ => reference::reference_rewrite(catalog, info, &rule.value),
            };
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#14.3 — a read-through child binds its parent's rule over hidden
        // captures (`.$enrollment.dancer`): its refusal is the parent's (`internal_names` drops the copy).
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.6 — a `read through` that did not expand (E_READ_THROUGH says why)
        // leaves its internal call; its refusal is that problem's cascade, spelled as written.
        if let Expr::Call { name, args, .. } = &rule.value && matches!(name.as_str(), "$readThrough" | "$readThroughLive") {
            let reference = match args.as_slice() { [Expr::Name { name, .. }] => name.clone(), _ => "parent".into() };
            error.message = format!("`read through .{reference}` did not expand, so {} has no read rule yet", info.decl.name);
            // The cascade convention: `check` hides it beside its cause unless --verbose.
            error.rewrite = "fix this Quarry line and rerun snapback4 check".into();
            error.cascade_of = Some(rule.site.clone());
            error.mechanical = false;
            return error;
        }
        if rule.effect == Effect::Read && let Some(through) = &info.through {
            error.rewrite = format!("repair {}'s read rule (its own diagnostic prints the repair); {} reads through it", through.parent, info.decl.name);
            error.cascade_of = Some(error.site.clone());
        }
        error
    })
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct RuleBindings {
    old: Option<String>,
    next: Option<String>,
    update: bool,
    insert: bool,
    hops: bool,
    present: Vec<RuleTerm>,
fn probe(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, site: &Site, bindings: &RuleBindings, extras: bool) -> Fallible<(String, String, Vec<RuleTerm>, Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>)> {
    if catalog.tables.get(&read.table).is_some_and(|t| t.decl.ephemeral.is_some()) {
        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL", format!("a rule or after insert cannot probe ephemeral table {}", read.table), "probe a durable table instead").guide("ephemeral"));
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — rules
    // and postconditions must not recover the compiler-owned physical row.
    if catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
        .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())
    {
        return Err(type_error(site, format!("{} is one global maintained scalar, not a rule-probe table", read.table),
            format!("read `{}` as a scalar in a query; keep rules over application tables", read.table)).guide("maintains"));
    }
    // `exists pieces[.id = .piece, …]`: a comparison where a key belongs. Name
    // the column (`id = .piece`) or give the full unique key by position first.
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — app-farm pattern 27: the positional unique-key form.
    for key in &read.keys {
        if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = &key.value && op == "="
            && let Expr::Member { base, name, .. } = left.as_ref() && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) {
            let others: Vec<String> = read.keys.iter().filter(|other| !std::ptr::eq(*other, key)).map(|other| match &other.column {
                Some(column) => format!("{column} = {}", crate::print::expression(&other.value)),
                None => crate::print::expression(&other.value),
            }).collect();
            let value = crate::print::expression(right);
            let positional = if name == "id" { std::iter::once(value.clone()).chain(others.iter().cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ") } else { std::iter::once(format!("{name} = {value}")).chain(others.iter().cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ") };
            return Err(unknown(&key.site, format!("`.{name} = {value}` is a comparison inside a probe key; in {}[..] a key names a column without a dot (`{name} = {value}`), or gives a full unique key by position first", read.table),
                format!("exists {}[{positional}]", read.table)).guide("rules"));
        }
    }
    let mixed = read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) && read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none());
    let types = if mixed { Some(read.keys.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).map(|key| rule_term(catalog, info, &key.value, bindings)
        .map(|(term, ty)| (ty, matches!(term, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)))))).collect::<Fallible<Vec<_>>>()?) } else { None };
    let normalized = catalog.tables.get(&read.table).map(|target| super::mixed_keys::read_typed(target, read, catalog, types.as_deref())).transpose()?;
    let read = normalized.as_deref().unwrap_or(read);
    super::validate_key_names(read)?;
    if read.range.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() { return Err(unknown(site, "a rule probe takes equalities, not a range", "exists t[key = .key]")); }
    let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) else {
        return Err(unknown(site, format!("exists probes unknown table {}", read.table), name_rewrite(&read.table, catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
    };
    for key in &read.keys {
        if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::row_views::predicate_field(target, field, site)?; }
    }
    let mut terms = Vec::new();
    let mut types = Vec::new();
    for key in &read.keys {
        let (term, ty) = rule_term(catalog, info, &key.value, bindings)?;
        terms.push((key.column.clone(), term));
        types.push(ty);
    }
    let named: Option<Vec<String>> = if terms.iter().all(|(c, _)| c.is_some()) && !terms.is_empty() { Some(terms.iter().map(|(c, _)| c.clone().unwrap()).collect()) } else { None };
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — an expiring maintain's physical value is not its live fact; only existence is a one-unit rule probe.
    if named.as_ref().is_some_and(|names| names.iter().any(|name| name == "value"))
        && catalog.maintains.get(&read.table).filter(|m| m.row_key.is_none()).and_then(|maintain| catalog.tables.get(&maintain.source)).is_some_and(|source| source.decl.expire.is_some() || source.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring)) {
        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_LIVE_MAINTAIN_PROBE", format!("a rule cannot probe {}.value because its source expires", read.table),
            "read the view in the program; its live value cannot be probed at one unit").guide("expire"));
    }
    if let Some(names) = &named {
        for ((_, term), (column, ty)) in terms.iter_mut().zip(names.iter().zip(&mut types)) {
            let expected = column_ty(target, column).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, target.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)))?;
            if !ty.comparable(&expected) && !(expected.is_identity() && matches!(term, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)))) { return Err(type_error(site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Vec<Invariant> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for decl in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.invariants) {
        let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&decl.table) else {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET",format!("invariant names unknown table {}",decl.table),"declare the table or correct its name").guide("security")); continue;
        };
        let named_operation=operation::resolve(catalog,decl);
        if (!matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && named_operation.is_none())
            || decl.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"&&!info.columns.contains_key(c)) {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET","invariant needs a declared column or table effect","use invariant table.read only <policy>, table.column write only <policy>, or table always <predicate>").guide("security")); continue;
        }
        let effect=match decl.target.as_str(){"insert"=>ast::Effect::Insert,"update"|"write"=>ast::Effect::Update,"delete"=>ast::Effect::Delete,_=>ast::Effect::Read};
        let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect,bindings:Vec::new(),value:decl.value.clone(),site:decl.site.clone()};
        let (policy,unsupported)=match super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule) {
            Ok(rule)=>(Some(rule),None),Err(error)=>match temporal::bind(catalog,info,&decl.value) {
                Some(rule) if decl.target=="read" => (Some(rule),None),
                _ => (None,Some(format!("policy outside rule proof fragment: {} ({})",error.message,error.code))),
            },
        };
        let requirements=named_operation.as_ref().map(|(_,p)| operation::requirements(catalog,info,p)).unwrap_or_default();
        out.push(Invariant{operation:named_operation.map(|(name,_)|name),requirements,syntax:Some(decl.value.clone()),table:decl.table.clone(),target:decl.target.clone(),column:decl.column.clone(),predicate:crate::print::expression(&decl.value),site:decl.site.clone(),policy,unsupported});
    }
    out
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="lowercase")]
pub enum Status { Proved, Refuted, Unproved }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Verdict {
    pub invariant: Invariant,
    pub status: Status,
    pub direct_rule: Status,
    pub paths: Vec<String>,
    pub reasons: Vec<String>,
    pub counterexample: Option<challenges::Counterexample>,
    pub obligations: Vec<Obligation>,
}
    let mut types = Vec::new();
    for key in &read.keys {
        let (term, ty) = rule_term(catalog, info, &key.value, bindings)?;
        terms.push((key.column.clone(), term));
        types.push(ty);
    }
    let named: Option<Vec<String>> = if terms.iter().all(|(c, _)| c.is_some()) && !terms.is_empty() { Some(terms.iter().map(|(c, _)| c.clone().unwrap()).collect()) } else { None };
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — an expiring maintain's physical value is not its live fact; only existence is a one-unit rule probe.
    if named.as_ref().is_some_and(|names| names.iter().any(|name| name == "value"))
        && catalog.maintains.get(&read.table).filter(|m| m.row_key.is_none()).and_then(|maintain| catalog.tables.get(&maintain.source)).is_some_and(|source| source.decl.expire.is_some() || source.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring)) {
        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_LIVE_MAINTAIN_PROBE", format!("a rule cannot probe {}.value because its source expires", read.table),
            "read the view in the program; its live value cannot be probed at one unit").guide("expire"));
    }
    if let Some(names) = &named {
        for ((_, term), (column, ty)) in terms.iter_mut().zip(names.iter().zip(&mut types)) {
            let expected = column_ty(target, column).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, target.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)))?;
//! Expanded helpers can write from any expression block.
// @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — legacy eligibility examines the complete expanded write footprint.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn stmts_write_tables(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Insert { table, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "insert")),
            Stmt::Update { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "update")),
            Stmt::Upsert { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "upsert")),
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, upsert, .. } => out.push((target.table.clone(), if *upsert { "upsert" } else { "update" })),
            Stmt::Delete { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "delete")),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => out.push((target.table.clone(), "delete")),
            _ => {}
        }
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_write_tables(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_write_tables(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_write_tables(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                stmts_write_tables(then_body, out);
                stmts_write_tables(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_write_tables(start, out); expr_write_tables(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_write_tables(&recurse.seed, out);
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_write_tables(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_write_tables(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                for key in &current.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_write_tables(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_write_tables(&condition.setup, out); expr_write_tables(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_write_tables(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_write_tables(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_write_tables(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
        match key { IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) | IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(expr) => expr_write_tables(expr, out) }
    }
    for cursor in [&scan.after, &scan.before].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(cursor, out); }
}

fn expr_write_tables(expr: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(_) => {}
        Expr::Field { base, .. } => expr_write_tables(base, out),
        Expr::Array(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr_write_tables(base, out); fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)); }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            expr_write_tables(left, out);
            expr_write_tables(right, out);
        }
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
        Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Get(read) | Expr::Exists(read) => {
            read.key.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out));
        }
        Expr::Block { body, value } => { stmts_write_tables(body, out); expr_write_tables(value, out); }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            if let Some(after) = merge.position.as_ref().and_then(|position| position.after.as_ref()) { expr_write_tables(after, out); }
            for lane in &merge.lanes {
                if let Some(guard) = &lane.guard { expr_write_tables(guard, out); }
                if let Some(project) = &lane.project { expr_write_tables(project, out); }
                if let Some(value) = &lane.distinct { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                stmts_write_tables(&lane.setup, out);
                if let Some(source) = &lane.source {
                    if let Some(scan) = source.scan() { scan_write_tables(scan, out); }
                    for value in source.expressions() { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                }
                scan_write_tables(&lane.scan, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

//! Typed server-clock facts used only by the proof abstraction, never runtime rules.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — lifetime is independent of physical sweep lag.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::BinaryOp as Op, schema::{Table, ColumnType}};
// Quarry identifiers cannot contain ':'. No authored field aliases this atom.
const CLOCK: &str = "$security:server-clock";
pub(super) fn clock() -> RuleTerm { RuleTerm::Field(CLOCK.into()) }
pub(super) fn has_clock(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
    rule.any_term(&|t| matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == CLOCK))
}
pub(super) fn comparison(op: Op, a: RuleTerm, b: RuleTerm) -> RuleExpr {
    match op {
        Op::Gt => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lt,b,a), Op::Gte => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lte,b,a),
        Op::Eq => RuleExpr::Eq(a,b), Op::Ne => RuleExpr::Ne(a,b),
        _ => RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b),
    }
}
pub(super) fn admission(table: &Table) -> RuleExpr {
    let mut facts = vec![table.rules.read.clone()];
    for (column,op) in table.not_before.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Lte)).chain(table.expire.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Gt))) {
        let field=RuleTerm::Field(column.clone());
        let edge=comparison(op,field.clone(),clock());
        let edge=if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(ColumnType::is_optional) {
            RuleExpr::Or(vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),edge])
        } else {edge};
        facts.push(edge);
    }
    RuleExpr::And(facts)
}

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, info: &super::super::catalog::TableInfo, expr: &ast::Expr) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    use ast::{Expr,Literal,TypeKind};
    fn term(info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,e:&Expr)->Option<RuleTerm>{match e {
        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now" => Some(clock()),
        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..}) && info.columns.get(name).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Time) => Some(RuleTerm::Field(name.clone())),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Number(n),..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n.parse().ok()?))),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Null,..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),
        _=>None,
    }}
    if let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=expr {
        if op=="and"||op=="or" {
            let xs=vec![bind(catalog,info,left)?,bind(catalog,info,right)?];
            return Some(if op=="and"{RuleExpr::And(xs)}else{RuleExpr::Or(xs)});
        }
        let op=match op.as_str(){"="=>Op::Eq,"!="=>Op::Ne,"<"=>Op::Lt,"<="=>Op::Lte,">"=>Op::Gt,">="=>Op::Gte,_=>return None};
        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(term(info,left),term(info,right)){return Some(comparison(op,a,b));}
    }
    if let Expr::Unary{op,value,..}=expr && op=="not" {return Some(RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(bind(catalog,info,value)?)));}
    let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:expr.clone(),site:expr.site().clone()};
    super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule).ok()
}
    let exposures=audit::exposures(bound);
    let mut verdicts=Vec::new();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let mut reasons=Vec::new();let mut direct=Status::Proved;
        if let Some(why)=&inv.unsupported {reasons.push(why.clone());direct=Status::Unproved;}
        else if inv.target=="always" {
            // This narrow theorem holds on every validated committed image,
            // including seed/adoption, with no assumption about a writer.
            let nonnull=match inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap() {
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))|
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null),RuleTerm::Field(f)) =>
                    table.columns.get(f).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_optional() && !matches!(t,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Json{..}) && !table.engine_field(f)),
                _=>false,
            };
            if !nonnull {direct=Status::Unproved; reasons.push("row assertion is not implied by the implemented required-column theorem; transitions, counts and time require an additional proof".into());}
        } else if inv.operation.is_some() {
            match operation::prove(bound,inv) {Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push("operation admission does not imply the policy; an actual operation challenge is required".into());},Err(e)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(e);}}
        } else {
            for word in effects(inv) {
                let admission=if word=="update" {table.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else if word=="read" {columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())}else{table.rules.for_effect(effect(word)).clone()};
                let implication=if word=="update"{images::update(table,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())}else{kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())};
                match implication {
                    Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word} admission does not imply intent over independent predicates; a concrete replay is required for refutation"));},
                    Err(why)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word}: {why}"));},
                }
                // Update implication judges both images. Only id/explicit
                // immutable local fields have the equal-image substitution;
                // owner-field and implicit writers remain separate blockers.
            }
        }
        reasons.extend(blockers(bound,inv));
        let obligations=observers::obligations(bound,inv);
        for o in &obligations {if o.status!=Status::Proved {reasons.push(format!("{}: {}; {}",o.path,o.reason,o.repair.as_deref().unwrap_or("declare an explicit observer policy")));}}
        let paths=exposures.iter().find(|x|x.table==inv.table).map(|x|x.paths.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
        let status=if direct==Status::Proved && reasons.is_empty(){Status::Proved}else{Status::Unproved};
        verdicts.push(Verdict{invariant:inv.clone(),status,direct_rule:direct,paths,reasons,counterexample:None,obligations});
    }
    let mut notes=lints::notes(bound,&exposures);
    let missing=adoption::missing(bound);
    if !missing.is_empty() {notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&Site::default(),"N_SECURITY_INTENT",format!("{} tables have access rules and no security invariants: {}; declare the spec's privacy rules as invariants (`guide security`)",missing.len(),missing.join(", ")),"review snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft against the spec; candidates are never auto-accepted").guide("security"));}
    let roles=audit::roles(&exposures);
    let uncovered=adoption::uncovered(bound,&exposures);
    Report{format:1,scope:"logical row/authority intent; all database snapshots; ordinary authenticated viewers including service seats; explicit unproved paths".into(),
        exclusions:vec!["system-principal trust boundary".into(),"physical timing, examined counts and resource/meter side channels".into(),"offline access may use a snapshot predating server revocation".into()],invariants:verdicts,exposures,roles,notes,uncovered,campaign_coverage:BTreeSet::new()}
}
fn blockers(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Vec<String> {
    let schema=&bound.schema;let t=&schema.tables[&inv.table];let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    if inv.target=="read" && inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,policy)!=Ok(true)) {
        out.insert("wire sequencing/activity metadata has no logical-existence reduction; direct row-rule proof does not establish whole-protocol noninterference".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy {
            let grants=policy.disjuncts().into_iter().filter(|r|!r.any_term(&|term|!matches!(term,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let global=RuleExpr::Or(grants);
            if !matches!(t.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) && kernel::implies(&t.rules.insert,&global)!=Ok(true) {
                out.insert("insert/unique/upsert collision outcomes can reveal an existing hidden row; old-row read authorization is not proved by new-image insert authority".into());
            }
            if kernel::implies(&t.update_rule_judged(),&policy.old_and_next())!=Ok(true) && !matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny) {
                out.insert("blind update/upsert outcomes can reveal hidden-target existence; read correspondence is unproved".into());
            }
        }
        if !t.written_by.is_empty(){out.insert("owner-field write outcomes can observe targets outside the row read audience".into());}
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.columns.iter().any(|(c,k)| matches!(k.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref{..}) && t.on_delete.get(c).copied().unwrap_or(snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Restrict).acts()) {
        out.insert("parent deletion/referential refusal can observe this child (including default restrict and transitive closures)".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.sync.is_some(){out.insert("device read uses a delivered snapshot; current server role at disconnected access is unproved".into());}
    if t.through.is_some(){out.insert("inherited/captured or live-parent authority requires a separate reduction (RULES5 owns live-parent semantics)".into());}
    if !schema.receipts.is_empty() || !schema.timelines.is_empty(){out.insert("receipt/timeline engine-owned paths require a separate reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());}
    if t.is_expiring() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("expiry/commit-end retirement can change rows outside ordinary delete rules".into());}
    if t.ephemeral.is_some() || !t.sequences.is_empty(){out.insert("ephemeral/sequence path is outside the current proof reduction".into());}
    if !t.written_by.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("owned-field update bypass: table rule alone does not establish field-write authority".into());}
    for (name,other) in &schema.tables {
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && other.on_delete.values().any(|a|matches!(a,snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascade|snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull)) && name==&inv.table {
            out.insert("incoming cascade/set-null changes bypass this table's ordinary rules".into());
        }
        if inv.target=="read" {
            for rule in [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete] {
                if kernel::dependencies(rule).contains(&inv.table) {out.insert(format!("raw rule probe/reference from {name} can observe {} independently of its read rule",inv.table));}
            }
        }
    }
    if schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.target==inv.table) && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") {out.insert("aggregate/view target rows are written by engine maintenance independently of their direct table rules".into());}
    if !t.maintained.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("engine-maintained columns bypass ordinary application write rules".into());}
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action)|p.program.native)
        || !schema.effects.is_empty() || !schema.action_specs.is_empty() {
        out.insert("system/job/action/native/external path requires a reviewed reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        for p in &bound.programs {
            let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && !census::body(&p.program.body) {out.insert(format!("{}: scan/merge/loop or opaque IR path needs a reviewed read-disclosure reduction",p.program.name));}
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(target,_)|target!=&inv.table) {
                out.insert(format!("{} may copy protected data into another table; cross-table disclosure reduction is unproved",p.program.name));
            }
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && audit::authored_refusal(&json) {out.insert(format!("{}: authored refusal observations are not reduced to this read policy",p.program.name));}
        }
    }
    out.into_iter().collect()
}

 pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
-    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
+    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c=="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
         if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation needs complete bound policy dependencies; unsupported policy AST is not dependency-free",inv.table));continue;}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown/unsupported is not false.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Literal};

fn boolean(v:Option<Value>)->Option<bool>{match v{Some(Value::Bool(v))=>Some(v),_=>None}}
pub(super) fn evaluate(expr:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<bool>{boolean(value(expr,row,rows,schema,viewer))}
fn value(e:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<Value>{Some(match e{
    Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"=>Value::Id(viewer.into()),
    Expr::Literal{value,..}=>match value{Literal::Str(s)=>Value::String(s.clone()),Literal::Bool(v)=>Value::Bool(*v),Literal::Null=>Value::Null,Literal::Number(s)=>Value::Int(s.parse().ok()?)},
    Expr::Focal{..}=>row.clone(),
    Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..})=>row.field(name).ok()?.clone(),
    Expr::Unary{op,value:x,..} if op=="not"=>Value::Bool(!boolean(value(x,row,rows,schema,viewer))?),
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"||op=="or"=>{
        let a=boolean(value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer));let b=boolean(value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer));
        let and=op=="and";let decisive=!and;
        Value::Bool(if a==Some(decisive)||b==Some(decisive){decisive}else if a.is_some()&&b.is_some(){and}else{return None;})
    },
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=>{
        let a=value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;let b=value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
        if matches!(op.as_str(),"+"|"-"|"*") {
            let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b)else{return None;};
            let n=match op.as_str(){"+"=>i128::from(a)+i128::from(b),"-"=>i128::from(a)-i128::from(b),_=>i128::from(a)*i128::from(b)};
            return Value::int(n).ok();
        }
        // The binder contextually types principal/id literals. This partial
        // evaluator refuses that coercion rather than using host strings.
        if !matches!((&a,&b),(Value::Id(_),Value::Id(_))|(Value::String(_),Value::String(_))|(Value::Int(_),Value::Int(_))|(Value::Bool(_),Value::Bool(_))|(Value::Null,_)|(_,Value::Null)){return None;}
        Value::Bool(match op.as_str(){"="=>a==b,"!="=>a!=b,"<"|"<="|">"|">="=>if let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b){match op.as_str(){"<"=>a<b,"<="=>a<=b,">"=>a>b,_=>a>=b}}else{return None;},_=>return None})
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if (name=="count"||name=="len")&&args.len()==1 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        Value::Int(values.len().try_into().ok()?)
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if name=="sum"&&args.len()==2 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        let sum=values.iter().try_fold(0i128,|sum,r|match value(&args[1],r,rows,schema,viewer)? {Value::Int(n)=>sum.checked_add(i128::from(n)),_=>None})?;
        Value::int(sum).ok()?
    },
    Expr::Array{items,..}=>Value::Array(items.iter().map(|e|value(e,row,rows,schema,viewer)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    Expr::Read(read)|Expr::Exists{read,..}=>{
        let t=schema.table(&read.table)?;
        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some()||t.through.is_some()||schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some()||read.filter.is_some()||read.range.is_some()||!read.seeks.is_empty()||read.matching.is_some(){return None;}
        let mut pins=Vec::new();
        for key in &read.keys{let column=key.column.as_ref()?;let val=value(&key.value,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
            let kind=t.columns.get(column)?;
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            let typed=match (kind.required(),&val) {
                (_,Value::Null)=>kind.is_optional(),
                (C::Id|C::Principal|C::Ref{..},Value::Id(_))=>true,
                (C::Text{..},Value::String(_))=>true,
                (C::Enum{variants},Value::String(v))=>variants.contains(v),
                (C::Int|C::IntRange{..}|C::Time,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (C::Bool,Value::Bool(_))=>true,_=>false,
            };
            if !typed{return None;}pins.push((column,val));}
        // Integrity assertions quantify over the complete raw snapshot, not
        // a caller-filtered program population. Temporal/captured populations
        // above remain unsupported. No solver proof uses these samples.
        let selected=rows.get(&read.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|pins.iter().all(|(c,v)|r.field(c).ok()==Some(v))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if matches!(e,Expr::Exists{..}) {Value::Bool(!selected.is_empty())}else{Value::Array(selected)}
    },

   131	            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],old)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
   132	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   133	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
   134	                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
   135	            }
   136	        }
   137	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   138	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],r));
   139	            if old==Some(row){continue;}
   140	            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
   141	            if inv.target!="always"&&inv.operation.is_none()&&!super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
   142	            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
   143	            if inv.target=="always" {
   144	                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
   145	            }else{
   146	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();

codex
**NOT READY**

1. **[P1] Reference operands are missing from write-stability dependencies.** [stability.rs:22](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/stability.rs:22) relies on `kernel::dependencies`, which records an `Exists` table but ignores references inside its keys/equalities. With intent and admission both `exists grants[.parent.owner]`, an operation can update the parent to an authorized owner and then insert. History judgment evaluates the old parent and falsely reports a reachable violation, failing acceptance.
   
   **Repair:** Include every operand’s reference hops in dependency collection, then apply the transitive authority closure before weakening or judging policies.

2. **[P2] Non-temporal read-hop intent never reaches authorized evaluation.** [security.rs:53](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security.rs:53) binds read invariants with runtime read-rule restrictions, which reject live reference hops. Consequently, `invariant notes.read only .parent.active = true` has no bound policy; [policy_in:102](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges.rs:102) falls back to the concrete evaluator, which also declines nested members. Even a public, readable parent with `active = false` cannot produce the promised fixture detection.
   
   **Repair:** Bind independent intent with authorized reference terms separately from runtime read-rule admissibility. Route those terms through `fixture_policy`, preserving Unknown for hidden/missing parents.

3. **[P2] Histories skip row assertions when another table changes.** [histories.rs:127](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/histories.rs:127) requires exactly one direct target write, then skips unchanged rows. For `reports always exists roles[.author, role = 'inspector']`, demoting the author changes only `roles`, so the newly false assertion is never checked.
   
   **Repair:** Evaluate `always` assertions over the committed snapshot after each history step, independently of write-attribution restrictions. Those restrictions belong to write-authority assertions.

4. **[P2] Helper-mediated role assignment bypasses escalation advice.** [lints.rs:103](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/lints.rs:103) scans original program bodies without expanding helper calls. Moving unrestricted role insertion into `fn enroll(role)` and calling it from `mutation join(role)` removes `N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION` while preserving identical authority.
   
   **Repair:** Analyze expanded caller writes with argument substitutions and dominating requirements, retaining source locations for the advisory.

5. **[P2] Constant defaults are incorrectly classified as caller-chosen roles.** [lints.rs:123](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/lints.rs:123) sets `dynamic = true` whenever the role field is omitted, including a resolved constant default. Ordinary fixed-member signup therefore receives escalation advice merely for acquiring member permissions; the equivalent explicit `'member'` assignment is exempted.
   
   **Repair:** Classify resolved constant defaults as fixed assignments and apply the same enrollment/delegation analysis as explicit literals.
